Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in separation theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 752 and the invariant I(A,B) = 282 mod 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by separation generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 282. QED.
